Simon Barrière | CV

Simon Barrière

Mechanical Fabrication Engineer at CERN

9+ years of experience
180+ fabrication projects launched in production
330+ contracts subcontracting follow-up
SRF / Precision Manufacturing core expertise

Profil

Mechanical Fabrication Engineer with strong experience in precision manufacturing, subcontracting strategy, assembly supervision, supplier coordination and production follow-up for advanced accelerator technologies.

I currently work within the CERN Engineering Department, where I manage precision mechanical fabrication and assembly projects linked to major programs such as HL-LHC WP4 and LHC Long Shutdown 3. My work includes technical coordination, supervision of manufacturing activities, industrial follow-up and fabrication process development for superconducting RF cavities and HOM couplers.

My background combines mechanical engineering, innovation management and hands-on experience with high-precision machining, sheet metal forming, brazing, welding, cryogenic systems, cleanroom procedures and project management in international environments.

Expérience professionnelle

June 2023 - Present

Mechanical Fabrication Engineer — CERN, Engineering Department, Mechanical & Materials Engineering Group (EN-MME)

Staff Member (STAF)

Responsible for managing precision mechanical fabrication and assembly projects, ensuring successful execution of production activities and coordination with industry partners for various CERN projects, especially HL-LHC WP4, preparation works for the LHC Long Shutdown 3 and activities with external institutes.

  • Lead technical teams and supervise assembly, installation and testing of complex mechanical systems.
  • Strong involvement in superconducting radio frequency cavities and high-order modes couplers production.
  • Oversee research and development efforts related to fabrication processes for accelerator technologies.
  • Focus on high-precision machining techniques across a wide range of materials.

Direct supervisor: M. Garlaschè, EN-MME-MA Section Leader.

August 2018 - July 2023

Fabrication & Subcontracting Engineer — CERN, Engineering Department, Mechanical & Materials Engineering Group (EN-MME)

External contractor (Sep. 2021 – May 2023) / Junior Fellow (Aug. 2018 – Aug. 2021)

Within CERN Central Mechanical Workshop, in charge of internal and external production of mechanical components for all CERN accelerators and experiments.

  • Provided engineering expertise in design for manufacturing and steered make-or-buy strategy.
  • Prepared 180+ projects representing about 5.9 MCHF and 65,000 workshop hours, including 70 as Project Manager (~3.5 MCHF).
  • Handled high-precision CNC machining, sheet metal forming & shaping, vacuum brazing, TIG/MIG, laser and electron-beam welding.
  • Acted as technical lead for EU-wide subcontracted manufacturing projects below and above 200k CHF.
  • Managed 330+ contracts and around 1.3 MCHF over 3 years.
  • Represented the CERN EN-MME group at conferences and industry exhibitions.
  • Responsible for component production and logistics linked to HL-LHC international collaborations.

Direct supervisor: A. Dallocchio, EN-MME-FS Section Leader.

January 2018 – July 2018

Field Coordinator — CERN, Beams Department, Radio Frequency Group (BE-RF)

Temporary worker (TEMC)

After contributing to the testing and assembly of the HL-LHC crab cavities prototype cryomodule, I joined the coordination team for its final installation in the SPS, the main injector of the LHC.

  • Coordinated and scheduled technical activities for infrastructure upgrades.
  • Collaborated with CERN staff and international contractors.
  • Ensured safety compliance for personnel and equipment, reporting to the Engineering Referent for Operational Safety.
  • Acted as liaison between the testing team and tunnel stakeholders.
  • Delivered weekly progress reports at the SPS Accelerator Coordination Committee.

Direct supervisor: G. Vandoni, BE-RF-SRF Physicist.

November 2016 - December 2017

Mechanical Project Engineer — CERN, Beams Department, Radio Frequency Group (BE-RF)

Temporary worker (TEMC)

Member of an international and multidisciplinary team working on the preparation and validation of superconducting RF cavities, especially HL-LHC crab cavities for SPS testing.

  • Developed cleanroom workflows and procedures for assembling superconducting RF cavities in ISO-4 and ISO-5 environments.
  • Led the design and managed manufacturing of mechanical systems for cryogenic tests at 2K using CATIA V5 / SmarTeam.
  • Maintained daily coordination with industrial suppliers.
  • Steered integration, planning and coordination of services and cabling teams for the HL-LHC cryomodule test bunker setup at SM18.
  • Provided technical support throughout the cavity testing program.

Direct supervisor: A. Macpherson, BE-RF-SRF Physicist.

Advanced Master in Innovation Management

EM Lyon Business School, Écully, France

Thesis: Organizational learning and change management: methods and guidelines.

September 2015

Master’s Degree in Mechanical Engineering

ICAM, Toulouse, France

Thesis: Mechanical characterization of TA6V titanium RCC pivots for space applications.

Compétences techniques

Precision manufacturing applied to accelerator technologies and components

Extensive experience with high-precision CNC multi-axis machining, sheet metal forming and shaping processes, including bending, stamping, vacuum brazing, TIG/MIG welding, and laser & electron-beam welding, applied to RF, cryogenic and ultra-high vacuum components.

Project & subcontracting management

Extensive experience in managing subcontracted manufacturing projects, drafting technical specifications, visiting suppliers to assess capabilities and quality systems, and ensuring timely delivery and conformity to requirements. Strong experience in logistics management for large-scale international collaborations.

Superconducting RF devices & related cryogenics systems

Experience with ISO-4 and ISO-5 cleanroom workflows for the assembly of superconducting RF cavities, contamination-free assembly and integration procedures, SRF cavity fabrication management and coordination of infrastructure upgrades for testing at 2K.

Computers and informatics

  • 2D & 3D Computer Aided Design (CAD)
  • Advanced level with CATIA V5
  • Advanced level with Creo Parametric
  • Advanced level with Microsoft Office: PowerPoint, Word, Excel

Additional skills

  • Coordination of multidisciplinary teams & contractors
  • Safety procedures & risk management in accelerator environments
  • Conference presentations & industry representation
